With 1.10.0 merged and no backfill yet, web asked restricted managers' jobs by `managerUserIds array-contains`, a field jobs saved before 1.10.0 do not have, so managers without the Projects / Small works toggle saw no jobs. While false, staff read whole job and booking collections as before 1.10.0 and the screens filter with `canSeeJob`. Deploy order: backfills → release with the flag true → rules deploy.

### 1.10.0 — 2026-11-02
- Owner decisions round 2 — jobs (§16): admins add and edit every job; managers add jobs, edit any job and change a job's manager list only with that kind's Projects / Small works add/edit/manage permission (`canCreateJob`, `canChangeJobManagers`, `canEditJob`, `JOB_MANAGER_LIST_FIELDS`, `jobWriteWithoutManagerList`); a manager without it edits the details of jobs they manage. `seesEveryJob` and `canSeeJob` take the kind and the toggles: a toggle shows every job of that kind. `canEditWorkCatalogue` no longer gates admins. Enforced by the rules on job create and update.
- New §17: reads are enforced. Managers without a toggle read only their jobs, those jobs' bookings and their own; operatives only their own bookings and their jobs by id. Both apps build their queries from `jobQueryPlan` / `bookingQueryPlan` (`jobIdQueryValues`, `readsEveryBooking`, `JOBS_PER_BOOKING_QUERY`, `OWN_BOOKING_IDS_PER_QUERY`); composite indexes added. Daily Overview and warnings take `warningScopeFor` (`scope` on `unbookedLabourRows`, `qualificationExpiryRows`, `unverifiedOperativeRows`, `materialCutOffWarningRows`) (§5, §6, §17).
- A manager never books themselves onto a job they do not manage (`canWriteBooking`, rules tested) (§16).
- `scripts/backfill-job-manager-user-ids.mjs` writes `managerUserIds` on existing jobs (Deploy notes, `docs/DEPLOY_1.10.0.md`).
- Tasks store `createdByUserId`; `task_completed` goes to `taskCompletedRecipients` (name fallback for older tasks). Scheduled push types are not pushed (no schedule in the push function) (§13).
- Hunt C: invoice rows are `timesheetInvoiceRows` (signed snapshot first, then reviews and entry decisions), payroll line reviews are `payrollLineReview` (any letter case), pay arithmetic is `payForHours` / `roundPennies` / `orgDayHours`, `payRateOnDay` matches every id of the person (`person`, `roster`), `timesheetParticipant` decides the Weekly Report feed and the reminder recipients, the Weekly Report never writes (`weeklyReport` trigger), and accepting an invitation is `inviteAcceptWrites` (role from the invitation; an existing account keeps its profile and the placeholder) (§7, §8, §11).
- Job visibility and booking permissions (owner decision; §2, §16). Admins see and book every job; managers see and create, edit, cancel and **delete** bookings only on the projects and small works they manage (`canSeeJob`, `visibleJobsFor`, `canWriteBooking`, `canDeleteBooking`, `isJobManager`, `jobManagerIds`, `stableManagerId`, `bookingJobId`); operatives never write bookings. `seesEveryJob`: admins, and (round 2, below) managers with that kind's toggle. Labour, manager site and subcontractor bookings and their tombstones are enforced by the security rules against the job document; jobs carry `managerUserIds` (`jobManagerUserIds`). Replaces "deleting a labour booking is admin only".
- Subcontractor bookings are tombstoned (`TOMBSTONED_BOOKING_COLLECTIONS`), and a job delete removes its future labour, subcontractor and job-linked manager site bookings (`jobDeleteBookingRemovals`) (§2).
- A role belongs to its organisation: the rules' manager checks (materials, wholesalers, variations, settings, warnings, holiday staff, send history, operative pay rows, invitations) read this organisation's role, never another company's users document (§14, §16).
- Deleting a person: `canDeleteUser` / `userDeleteRefusal` gate first, `userDeleteWrites` is the one ordered plan (users document, tax ids, membership, email claim and members entry in one batch first; then future bookings with tombstones, roster rows, invitations) (§11).
- One membership shape: `parseOrgMembershipDocument` / `orgMembershipWriteFields`; a person can no longer raise their own membership role or permissions (§14, §16).
- Inbox rows count from their `createdAt` (`inboxNotificationVisibleToUser` / `unreadNotificationBadgeCount` take `now`; push function too) (§13).
- Pay changes: `payRateChangeWrites` — the seed row only when history loaded and is empty, written in the profile's batch; a failed history load refuses the save (§8).
- Notifications: `notificationRelatedIdFor` is the one relatedId source; variation destinations; web writes `toolbox_talk_issued`, `task_created`, `task_completed`, `booking_created`; new `holiday_cancelled_by_manager` and `holiday_booked_for_you` instead of reusing decision types; self-book off writes nothing; the §13 Status column is current (§13).
- Weekly report: a dismissed clash is not open (`isWarningActive`) (§7). `leaveRecordsForAccount` matches leave stored with the account email as `userId` (§10).
- §1 exception for the idempotent warning-key migration (`IDEMPOTENT_MIGRATION_WRITES`, `syncWriteAllowed(trigger, migration)`); job type saves are explicit adds and removes only (`jobTypesChangeWriteFields`).
- Cross-platform contract (`cross-platform-contract.json`, `readCrossPlatformSample`), mirrored to iOS; a write-shape change updates it (§1, "How the rulebook changes").
- Live leftovers from 1.9.0: Daily Overview reads organisation settings from the live organisation listener; the weekly report's subcontractor bookings and the org-wide materials and send records (Home, Warnings) are live. A payment-run save builds `paymentRunHistory` from the server copy, never the screen draft (§4.5).

### 1.9.0 — 2026-11-02
- Live screens everywhere (§1): `LIVE_COLLECTIONS` lists every shared collection and document and whether a screen showing it listens (`liveCollection`, `screenMustListen`; only picker suggestions, tax ids, the platform toolbox library and tombstones may load on open). Before, iOS loaded projects, clients, operatives, managers, tasks, job types and annual leave only at launch or on pull-to-refresh, and web read most collections behind a 60-second cache and loaded My Schedule leave once per session (against §10), so edits on one platform did not appear on the other. Listeners are scoped to the open organisation and removed on a switch.
- Never re-save what you didn't change (§1): only a user action or its offline replay writes (`SYNC_WRITE_TRIGGERS`, `syncWriteAllowed`); a save writes only the changed records (`recordsToSave`); an edit of a missing document is refused, never re-created (`editOfMissingDocumentIsRefused`, `replayRefusedIsFinal`, `EDIT_OF_MISSING_DOCUMENT_MESSAGE`). iOS re-saved every in-memory project, small work and client on each return to the foreground, writing stale values over web edits and re-creating jobs and clients deleted on web.
- Closed 1.8.0 gaps: a deliberate removal of the last health & safety row (`healthSafetyDocumentWriteFields` `removedIds`) or job type (`jobTypesWriteFields` `removed`) can be saved; removing a recipient or issue removes its signatures (`healthSafetySignatureRemovalWrites`); shared writers for `variationTrackers` (`variationTrackerWriteFields`), `settings/variationTrades` (`parseVariationTrades`, `variationTradesWriteFields`) and settings-log entries without an id (`variationLogItems`, `variationLogItemId`, `parseVariationLogDocument`, `variationLogWriteFields`); `annualLeavePendingOmitFields`; notification `relatedId` per type (`NOTIFICATION_RELATED_ID`, `notificationRelatedId`; iOS minted `UUID()`), and create shapes for inbox rows (`notificationCreateFields`, never rewriting an existing row's read state), invitations (`invitationCreateFields`, `invitationUsedWriteFields`), organisations (`organizationCreateFields`) and tax ids (`taxIdsDocumentFields`).
- Security rules (§16): a staff material status write may carry `updatedBy` / `updatedFrom` (validated); `dismissedWarnings` writes are admins and managers only, as §16 already said. Needs a rules deploy.
- Bug hunt fixes: timesheet saves diff the sheet as loaded (`timesheetChangedFields`; a reviewer save on web wiped an owner's signature, expenses or price work saved on iOS, and the owner path read "manager signed" from a stale copy) (§8); Weekly Report leave days are `leaveDaysInRange` on `leaveRecordDayKeys` (§7); a decision on a leave cancellation notifies the requester (§9); manager site booking edits are partial (`managerSiteBookingUpdateWriteFields`; web merge-set the whole booking and re-stamped `createdAt`) and booking creates carry write metadata (§1); My Schedule and annual leave match every id of the person (`aliasIds`, `leaveRecordsForAccount`) (§9, §10); clash approval writes are one batch (§5). Web also normalised booking dates to London midnight instead of the organisation zone (§1, app fix).

### 1.8.0 — 2026-11-02
- One reader per operations and record collection (§1): `parseMaterialLineDocument` / `parseMaterialListDocuments`, `parseMaterialCatalogueDocument`, `parseMaterialSendRecordDocument`, `parseWholesalerDocument`, `parseSiteAuditDocument`, `parseTaskDocument`, `parseJobTypesDocument` (with `coerceJobTypeList` / `canonicalJobTypeName` moved into the rulebook), `parseTradeTypeInventory`, `parseOperativeProfileDocument`, `parseHealthSafetyDocument`, `parseVariationDocument`, `parseVariationTrackerDocument`, `parseNotificationDocument`, `parseWarningResolutionDocument`. iOS dropped material lines, send records, wholesalers, site audits, tasks and inbox rows whose ids were not UUIDs or whose unit, type or status it did not know; both apps minted random ids for task items and variation lines stored without one. The §1 known-gaps list is empty.
- Partial writes for every one of them (§1): material lines (organisation-zone `date` plus `dayKey`; iOS used device midnight), the material catalogue (`materialCatalogueWrites`; web deleted and rewrote the whole catalogue), tasks (both apps did a whole-task `setData`), wholesalers, site audits (web re-stamped `createdAt` and dropped `imageCapturedAt`), variations and renumbering, the `operativeProfiles` mirror, `settings/jobTypes`, `settings/tradeTypeInventory`.
- Health & safety: one issue-status vocabulary (web wrote `complete`, iOS read only `completed`; finished web issues showed as awaiting on iOS) — issues are stored `completed`. Signatures are per-signer map entries (`signatureRecords`, `healthSafetySignatureWriteFields`) so concurrent signatures are never lost; whole-list saves keep the other app's fields and never replace a list with an empty one.
- Notifications (§13): every app reads inbox rows with `parseNotificationDocument` and accepts every `KNOWN_NOTIFICATION_TYPES` type; the iOS enum lacks `variation` and `variation_renumbered`, and iOS read `relatedId` only as a UUID.
- Warning resolutions (§5): live listener on web; legacy iOS keys read in their shared form (`sharedWarningResolutionKey`) and the D3 migration is the shared `warningResolutionMigrationWrites`.
- Job keys (§1): job-scoped records store the job's document id as stored; settings documents use the upper-cased key (`healthSafetyDocumentId`, like `deadlinesDocumentId`); keys map back with `storedJobIdForKey`; a job with a client name but no client id gets `jobClientId`. Contacts and rows stored without an id get `derivedRowId` ids (`subcontractorContactId`, `wholesalerContactId`, `variationLineId`). Organisation references stored as a path or DocumentReference read as the id (`documentOrganizationId`); `parseUserDocument` marks `annualLeaveDefaultsPending` before the organisation loads.
- `subcontractorBookingCreateFields` writes clock times whenever given (comment corrected; code unchanged).

### 1.7.0 — 2026-11-02
- One reader per work collection (§1): `parseJobDocument` / `parseProjectDocument` / `parseSmallWorkDocument` (`projects`, `smallWorks`), `parseSubcontractorBookingDocument`, `parseTimesheetEntries`, `parseDeadlineDocument` / `parseDeadlineItem`, `parseDeadlineAssignments` / `deadlineAssignedProjectIds`. Ids are the document id or the stored id in stored case and a non-UUID id is kept (iOS randomised or dropped non-UUID job, client, manager, subcontractor-booking, entry and deadline ids; web dropped non-UUID deadline ids). A job with no start date starts on its creation day (web used "now"); no end date ends on its start day.
- Subcontractor bookings created on web are now real records on iOS: the document id is an upper-case UUID also stored as `id`, with `status`, `isBreakRemoved` and the contact lists always written (`subcontractorBookingCreateFields`); the reader accepts older web documents with no `id` or `status` (Confirmed) or slot (FULL DAY) (§1).
- Timesheet price work and expense entries keep their stored ids; an entry without one gets the deterministic `timesheetEntryId` (web minted a new random id on every read, so `extraEntryReviews[entryId]` never matched) (§8).
- Partial writes for work documents (§1): `jobWriteFields`, `bookingUpdateWriteFields` (web no longer rewrites the whole booking on an edit; an edit of a deleted booking is refused), `subcontractorBookingWriteFields`, and `deadlineAssignmentWriteFields` (a field-path update; never a merge-set with a dotted key, which iOS stored as a literal `projects.{KEY}` field — `parseDeadlineAssignments` still reads those).
- Deleting a person or a job removes only bookings from today on, each with its tombstone; past bookings stay; deactivating removes nothing (`bookingsToRemoveOnOperativeDelete`, `bookingsToRemoveOnProjectDelete`) (§2). iOS deleted every booking of a deleted operative without tombstones.

### 1.6.0 — 2026-11-02
- One reader per people collection (§1): `parseUserDocument` (`users/{uid}`), `parseOperativeDocument`, `parseManagerDocument`, `parsePayRateHistoryDocument` / `parsePayRateHistoryDocuments`, `parseClientDocument`, `parseSubcontractorDocument`, `parseSkillDocument`, with `documentInstantMs`, `documentBool`, `documentNumber`, `normalizeUserRoleToken`, `USER_DOCUMENT_KEYS`, `ROSTER_DOCUMENT_KEYS`. Ids are the document id in stored case: iOS randomised or upper-cased non-UUID `operatives` ids and dropped non-UUID `clients` and `operativeDayRateHistory` rows. `users/{uid}` and `operatives` leave the known-gaps list.
- An hourly `operatives` / `managers` row is hourly on every platform (iOS read `dayRate ?? hourlyRate` and ignored `payBasis`) (§8).
- Missing user leave fields take the organisation `annualLeaveDefaults` (iOS used 25 / 1 / 12) (§4.4, §8). Missing `timesheetsEnabled` is `defaultTimesheetsEnabled`: operatives and self-employed on, PAYE managers and admins off (web read off) (§8).
- An admin with a stray `operativeMode` flag stays an admin on lists; the approval rules keep reading the stored flag (`adminOutsideOperativeMode`) (§8).
- Partial writes for people (§1, §11): `documentWriteFields`, `userProfileWriteFields`, `operativeRowWriteFields`, `managerRowWriteFields`, `clientWriteFields`, `subcontractorWriteFields` → `{ set, remove }`; a field the stored copy lacks is never deleted; line manager ids with `hasNoLineManager`, pay basis with both rates and employment type with its transition are written together. Web Operative / Manager forms no longer overwrite the whole `users/{uid}` document and web roster, client and subcontractor saves are partial updates. The skills catalogue is saved with `skillsCatalogueWrites` (no delete-all and rewrite).
- Still each app's own reader (known gaps): `settings/jobTypes`, `settings/tradeTypeInventory`.

### 1.5.0 — 2026-11-02
- One reader for annual leave documents: `parseHolidayDocument` / `parseHolidayDocuments` (with `holidayDocumentDayKey`, `labourHolidayInput`, `HOLIDAY_DOCUMENT_KEYS`). Web and iOS read `holidayBookings` with different keys, status words, id rules and day conversion, so the same record fed `unbookedLabourRows` different inputs (iOS listed an admin on approved leave as unbooked). Every platform now reads leave only through this function and passes its organisation-zone day keys to warnings and Daily Overview (§9).
- Status synonyms are shared (`HOLIDAY_STATUS_SYNONYMS`, `normalizeHolidayStatus`): declined, denied and cancelled are rejected and requested / awaiting / submitted are pending (web read them as approved). Slot synonyms morning / afternoon read as AM / PM (`normalizeHolidayTimeSlot`) (§9).
- Unbooked labour and leave coverage match ids without case (`normalizeDocumentId`): an iOS upper-case `userId`, `operativeId` or booking `personId` matches a web lower-case id and the reverse, so leave and bookings are no longer missed by letter case; returned rows keep the stored ids (`unbookedLabourRows`, `unbookedLabourRowsOnDay`, `leaveCoverageRows`) (§5).
- §1 lists the shared document readers; `users/{uid}` documents and `operatives` roster rows are known gaps without one.

### 1.4.0 — 2026-11-02
- Bank-holiday regions are one shared list (`BANK_HOLIDAY_REGIONS`, `bankHolidayRegionsForCountry`, `defaultBankHolidayRegionForCountry`, `normalizeBankHolidayRegionId`). Changing the company country asks for that country's region and the save waits for it (`companyDetailsRegionPrompt`, `companyDetailsValidationError`); Annual leave defaults offer only the company country's regions. Existing leave is never recounted (§4.1, §4.4).
- Company activity notices (`operative_created`, `manager_created`, `client_created`, `project_created`, `small_works_created`, `warning_removed`) go to the active admins outside operative mode, never the actor, one row each (`companyActivityRecipients`); admins get a Company activity toggle (`companyActivity`) and the six per-type toggles are retired (§13).
- `push_test` (Settings "send a test notification") reaches its sender: `pushTestRecipients`, `notificationPushesToActor`, and the push function's actor guard skips it (§13).
- Noted: iOS forced sign-outs do not yet remove the push token (§13).

### 1.3.0 — 2026-11-02
- Every operative and manager needs an active line manager, whatever the self-book permission or annual leave setting; only admins and super admins outside operative mode may have none (`lineManagerRequired`, `lineManagerRequiredError`, "Set a line manager — this person's timesheets and annual leave need approval.") (§8, §11).
- An admin or super admin with no active line manager is self-approved: their timesheet is self-signed (`timesheetSelfSigned`) and their leave saves approved behind a "Submit" button (`canSelfBookAnnualLeave(user, roster)`, `leaveSubmitLabel`). An admin with a line manager follows normal approval (§8, §9).
- Line managers and approvers are active only and never the person under any of their ids (`lineManagerIds(user, roster)`, `personIdSet`). With no line manager only admins decide — never any manager (`leaveApproverIds`, `canDecideLeave`, rules `hbCanDecide`) (§8, §9, §16).
- One recipient rule per notification type, never the actor (`notificationRecipients.ts`, `excludeActor`); `NOTIFICATION_CATALOG` is the §13 table with known app divergences in its Status column (§13).
- Inbox rows carry `actorUserId` (validated by the rules); the push function never pushes to the actor; the badge counts only the recipient's own unread rows — ownerless rows are not inbox rows (`unreadNotificationBadgeCount`) (§13, §16).
- Timesheet notice de-dupe keys use the organisation-zone day (`timesheetNotificationDedupeKey`). A device push token belongs to the signed-in account only and is removed from every users document on sign-out (§13).

### 1.2.0 — 2026-10-31
- Booking tombstones carry only their own fields and are the one exception to write metadata (§1, §2).
- Annual leave: self-booking is the `annualLeaveSelfBook` permission outside operative mode only (`canSelfBookAnnualLeave`). Anyone who does not self-book must have a current active line manager (`lineManagerRequiredError`, `usersMissingLineManager`). Nobody decides their own request; admins decide any request (`canDecideLeave`, `leaveDecisionQueue`). Holiday deletes follow the same deciders (§8, §9, §11, §16).
- Operative clashes come from `operativeClashGroups`, one approval pair per overlapping pair; a cluster is approved only when every pair is (`isClashGroupApproved`). Managers may un-approve (§5, §16).
- Only approvals count as approved clashes (`approvedWarningKeys`); a dismissed clash is hidden but not approved (§5).
- Document ids keep their stored case and compare without case (`normalizeDocumentId`) for warning keys, clash pairs and timesheet entry reviews (§1).
- Offline: the §2 table is exported (`OFFLINE_ACTIONS`, `offlineAvailability`, the three messages). Deleting a booking is online only; a not-found edit replay is final (`bookingReplayRefusedIsFinal({ code, isCreate })`). Labour booking deletes stay admin only (§2, §16).
- `unbookedScanDays`, `warningLookaheadFallbackNotice` and `isPlatformOwnerEmail` are in the packed script (§5, §14).
- Clearing a rate writes a "rate not set" history row, and history days are organisation-zone midnight (`payRateHistoryWriteFields`) (§8).
- PAYE and leave lines never appear on any invoice (`invoiceLines`) (§8).
- `timesheetOwnerWriteFields` drops sign-off and export fields itself; owner saves and leave edits are partial updates (§1, §8, §9).
- `ORGANIZATION_DOCUMENT_FIELDS` and `OPERATIONAL_COLLECTION_PATHS` are in the packed script; `profilePhotoUpdatedAt` is a user field (§1, §12).
- Documented: the live roster is the `organizationId` query; the legacy literal `settings.currencyCode` field is left in place (§1, §4.2, §11).

### 1.1.0 — 2026-10-31
- My notifications toggles stop the device push only; the inbox row and unread count always show the notice (`notificationShowsInInbox`, `devicePushAllowed`). The app-icon badge matches the inbox.
- Every variation notification type sends a device push (`variation_renumbered`, `variation_added`, `variation_from_tracker`, `variation_numbers_updated` join `variation`).
- Pay and manager decisions are protected on every path (security rules and shared rules; see §8 and §16).

### 1.0.0 — 2026-10-31
- First versioned rulebook. Consolidates the rules previously spread across `docs/CANONICAL_ARCHITECTURE.md`, `AGENTS.md` and the iOS copies into this one file, mirrored to iOS.
- Decisions: organisation currency used for every money figure (11 currencies, country default); one business time zone per organisation from its country; fixed Monday–Sunday week; scheduled working-hours changes on every platform; recurring payment runs paid in arrears after the work period; weekend clashes checked when a weekend booking exists; overlapping bookings each paid and shown in amber; PAYE days shown at 0; annual leave shown on timesheets as information (never on invoices); one leave document per request with a decision note; weekly reports never stored; booking tombstones stop offline resurrection; password minimum 8; Variations notification toggle; write metadata `updatedBy` / `updatedFrom`.
